Screens:
  Contracts Screen:
    Properties:
      Fill: |+
        =gblAppStyles.Screen.Fill
      LoadingSpinner: =LoadingSpinner.Data
      OnHidden: |
        =Clear(colBoPContracts);
      OnVisible: |-
        =UpdateContext({locContractScreenLoading:true});

        UpdateContext(
            {
                locContractsCostsMax: 1000000000,
                locSelectedContract: First(
                    FirstN(
                        'BoP Projects Contracts',
                        0
                    )
                ),
                locSelectedContractPaymentTarget: First(
                    FirstN(
                        'BoP Contracts Payment Targets',
                        0
                    )
                ),
                locSelectedCapexAccountsForContract: First(
                    FirstN(
                        colCapexAccountCategoriesNew,
                        0
                    )
                ),
                locSelectedCapexProjectContract: First(
                    FirstN(
                        colCapexCostsInSelectedContracts,
                        0
                    )
                ),
                locSelectedContractType: Blank(),
                locContractDevCoCostsCahnged: false,
                locContractDevCoCostsDirty: false,
                locIsVisibleRightPanelNewEditContract: false,
                locIsVisibleRightPanelNewEditRightsContract: false,
                locIsVisibleRightPanelNewEditContractPaymentTarget: false,
                locContractSpinnerInformationText: "Saving Contract data...",
                locIsContractVisiblePopUpSpinner: false,
                locIsVisiblePopUpDeleteContract: false,
                locIsVisiblePopUpDeleteContractPaymentTarget: false
            }
        );
        ClearCollect(
            colBoPContracts,
            AddColumns(
                Filter(
                    'BoP Projects Contracts',
                    Project.Project = gblSelectedProject.Project
                ),
                IsFolded,
                true
            )
        );
        ClearCollect(
            colBoPCapexProjectContracts,
            Filter(
                'CAPEX Project Contracts',
                And(
                    Project.Project = gblSelectedProject.Project,
                    'Cost Type' = 'Cost paid type'.DevCo
                )
            )
        );
        ClearCollect(
            colBoPStandardContractsFabric,
            Filter(
                'Assumptions BoP Contracts',
                And(
                    countryname = gblSelectedProject.Country.Name,
                    technology = Text(gblSelectedProject.Technology)
                )
            )
        );
        With(
            {
                varColCapexProjectContracts: ShowColumns(
                    colBoPCapexProjectContracts,
                    'CAPEX Project Contract'
                )
            },
            ClearCollect(
                colSelectedProjectCapexContractsCosts,
                SortByColumns(
                    ShowColumns(
                        AddColumns(
                            Filter(
                                'CAPEX Costs',
                                And(
                                    ThisRecord.Contract.'CAPEX Project Contract' in varColCapexProjectContracts,
                                    Not(ThisRecord.Contract.'Cost Type' = 'Cost paid type'.SPV)
                                )
                            ),
                            cn,
                            ThisRecord.Name,
                            mm,
                            Int(ThisRecord.Month),
                            type,
                            Text(ThisRecord.Contract.'Cost Type')
                        ),
                        cn,
                        'CAPEX Cost',
                        Contract,
                        Cost,
                        type,
                        Month,
                        mm,
                        Year
                    ),
                    "cn",
                    SortOrder.Ascending,
                    "vsb_year",
                    SortOrder.Ascending,
                    "mm",
                    SortOrder.Ascending
                )
            )
        );
        ClearCollect(
            colContractsDevCoCosts,
            FirstN(
                'BoP Contracts DevCo Costs',
                0
            )
        );
        ClearCollect(
            colCapexAllAccounts,
            Blank()
        );
        ClearCollect(
            colCapexAllAccountsTemporary,
            'CAPEX Account Lists'
        );
        ClearCollect(
            colSelectedConratctWithDevCoCosts,
            colCapexAllAccounts
        );
        With(
            {
                varColParentAccounts: Filter(
                    colCapexAllAccountsTemporary,
                    And(
                        'Parent Account'.Number = "00001",
                        Not(ThisRecord.Number = "10006")
                    )
                )
            },
            ForAll(
                varColParentAccounts,
                With(
                    {
                        varId: ThisRecord.'CAPEX Account List',
                        varParentId: ThisRecord.'CAPEX Account List',
                        varName: ThisRecord.Name,
                        varNummer: ThisRecord.Number,
                        varOrder: ThisRecord.Order
                    },
                    With(
                        {
                            varChilds: Filter(
                                colCapexAllAccountsTemporary,
                                'Parent Account'.'CAPEX Account List' = varId
                            )
                        },
                        Collect(
                            colCapexAllAccounts,
                            {
                                Id: varParentId,
                                ParentId: Blank(),
                                ParentIdLevel1: Blank(),
                                ParentIdLevel2: Blank(),
                                Nummer: varNummer,
                                Name: varName,
                                TotalCost: 0,
                                Order: varOrder,
                                Level: 1,
                                ChildsCount: CountIf(
                                    varChilds,
                                    true
                                ),
                                ContractDevCo: EmptyGUID,
                                Selected: false,
                                UsedInContractDevCo: EmptyGUID,
                                Used: false,
                                IsFolded: true
                            }
                        );
                        ForAll(
                            varChilds,
                            With(
                                {
                                    varId: ThisRecord.'CAPEX Account List',
                                    varParentIdLevel1: ThisRecord.'CAPEX Account List',
                                    varName: ThisRecord.Name,
                                    varNummer: ThisRecord.Number,
                                    varOrder: ThisRecord.Order
                                },
                                With(
                                    {
                                        varChildsLevel2: Filter(
                                            colCapexAllAccountsTemporary,
                                            'Parent Account'.'CAPEX Account List' = varId
                                        )
                                    },
                                    Collect(
                                        colCapexAllAccounts,
                                        {
                                            Id: varId,
                                            ParentId: varParentId,
                                            ParentIdLevel1: varParentIdLevel1,
                                            ParentIdLevel2: Blank(),
                                            Nummer: varNummer,
                                            Name: varName,
                                            TotalCost: 0,
                                            Level: 2,
                                            Order: varOrder,
                                            ChildsCount: CountIf(
                                                varChildsLevel2,
                                                true
                                            ),
                                            ContractDevCo: EmptyGUID,
                                            Selected: false,
                                            UsedInContractDevCo: EmptyGUID,
                                            Used: false,
                                            IsFolded: true
                                        }
                                    );
                                    ForAll(
                                        varChildsLevel2,
                                        With(
                                            {
                                                varId: ThisRecord.'CAPEX Account List',
                                                varParentIdLevel2: ThisRecord.'CAPEX Account List',
                                                varName: ThisRecord.Name,
                                                varNummer: ThisRecord.Number,
                                                varOrder: ThisRecord.Order
                                            },
                                            With(
                                                {
                                                    varChildsLevel3: Filter(
                                                        colCapexAllAccountsTemporary,
                                                        'Parent Account'.'CAPEX Account List' = varId
                                                    )
                                                },
                                                Collect(
                                                    colCapexAllAccounts,
                                                    {
                                                        Id: varId,
                                                        ParentId: varParentId,
                                                        ParentIdLevel1: varParentIdLevel1,
                                                        ParentIdLevel2: varParentIdLevel2,
                                                        Nummer: varNummer,
                                                        Name: varName,
                                                        TotalCost: Sum(
                                                            Filter(
                                                                colBoPCapexProjectContracts,
                                                                Account.'CAPEX Account List' = varId
                                                            ),
                                                            'Total Cost'
                                                        ),
                                                        Level: 3,
                                                        Order: varOrder,
                                                        ChildsCount: CountIf(
                                                            varChildsLevel3,
                                                            true
                                                        ),
                                                        ContractDevCo: EmptyGUID,
                                                        Selected: false,
                                                        UsedInContractDevCo: EmptyGUID,
                                                        Used: false,
                                                        IsFolded: true
                                                    }
                                                )
                                            )
                                        )
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        )
                    )
                )
            )
        );

        UpdateContext({locContractScreenLoading:false});
    Children:
      - con_Contracts_Screen:
          Control: GroupContainer@1.5.0
          Variant: AutoLayout
          Properties:
            BorderStyle: =BorderStyle.None
            DropShadow: =DropShadow.None
            Height: =Parent.Height
            LayoutDirection: =LayoutDirection.Vertical
            LayoutGap: =5
            LayoutOverflowY: =LayoutOverflow.Scroll
            PaddingBottom: =gblAppSizes.Padding.Page
            Width: =Parent.Width
            X: |-
              =If(
                  App.Width < 1920,
                  gblAppSizes.Padding.Page,
                  Max((App.Width-Self.Width)/2,gblAppSizes.Padding.Page)
              )
            Y: =gblAppSizes.Padding.Page
          Children:
            - cmp_Contracts_Header:
                Control: CanvasComponent
                ComponentName: cmp_Header
                Properties:
                  ApprovalStatesColor: =gblTableApprovalStateColors
                  Height: =70
                  LayoutMaxHeight: =
                  LayoutMaxWidth: =
                  LogoWidth: =210
                  MainScreen: =App.ActiveScreen
                  OnLogoClick: |-
                    =//Navigate('Capex Costs Screen',ScreenTransition.Fade);
                    Launch(gblPMAppURL)
                  Project: =gblProjectHeaderData
                  Width: =Parent.Width
            - con_Contracts_Body:
                Control: GroupContainer@1.5.0
                Variant: AutoLayout
                Properties:
                  AlignInContainer: =AlignInContainer.SetByContainer
                  DropShadow: =DropShadow.None
                  Fill: =Color.Transparent
                  Height: =Parent.Height-Self.Y-con_Contracts_Footer.Height-20
                  LayoutDirection: =LayoutDirection.Horizontal
                  LayoutGap: =5
                  LayoutMaxHeight: =
                  LayoutMaxWidth: =
                  PaddingLeft: =6
                  PaddingRight: =6
                  RadiusBottomLeft: =0
                  RadiusBottomRight: =0
                  RadiusTopLeft: =0
                  RadiusTopRight: =0
                  Width: =Parent.Width
                Children:
                  - con_Contracts_LeftNavigationMenu:
                      Control: GroupContainer@1.5.0
                      Variant: ManualLayout
                      Properties:
                        AlignInContainer: =AlignInContainer.SetByContainer
                        Fill: =Color.White
                        FillPortions: =0
                        Height: =Parent.Height
                        LayoutMaxHeight: =
                        LayoutMaxWidth: =
                        LayoutMinWidth: =200
                        Width: =If(gblLeftNavigationMenuIsExpanded, gblAppSizes.LeftNavigation.ExpandedWidth,gblAppSizes.LeftNavigation.DecreasedWidth)
                      Children:
                        - cmp_Contracts_Left_Navigation:
                            Control: CanvasComponent
                            ComponentName: cmp_Left_Navigation
                            Properties:
                              FontSize: =gblAppSizes.Font.Medium
                              Height: =Parent.Height
                              IsExpanded: =gblLeftNavigationMenuIsExpanded
                              Items: =LeftNavigationMenu
                              OnExpand: |-
                                =Set(
                                    gblLeftNavigationMenuIsExpanded,
                                    Not(gblLeftNavigationMenuIsExpanded)
                                );
                              OnSelect: |
                                =If(
                                    Self.SelectedKey <> gblLeftNavigationSelected.ItemKey,
                                    Set(
                                        gblLeftNavigationSelected,
                                        LookUp(
                                            LeftNavigationMenu,
                                            ItemKey = Self.SelectedKey
                                        )
                                    );
                                    Navigate(
                                        gblLeftNavigationSelected.TargetScreen,
                                        ScreenTransition.Fade
                                    )
                                );
                              Selected: =gblLeftNavigationSelected
                              Width: =Parent.Width
                  - con_Contracts_Content:
                      Control: GroupContainer@1.5.0
                      Variant: AutoLayout
                      Properties:
                        AlignInContainer: =AlignInContainer.SetByContainer
                        Fill: =ColorValue(gblAppTheme.palette.white)
                        Height: =Parent.Height-Self.Y
                        LayoutDirection: =LayoutDirection.Vertical
                        LayoutMaxHeight: =
                        LayoutMaxWidth: =
                        PaddingLeft: =20
                        PaddingRight: =10
                        Visible: =Not(IsBlank(gblSelectedProject))
                        Width: =Parent.Width
                      Children:
                        - con_Contracts_Content_CommandBar:
                            Control: GroupContainer@1.5.0
                            Variant: ManualLayout
                            Properties:
                              AlignInContainer: =AlignInContainer.SetByContainer
                              BorderStyle: =BorderStyle.None
                              DropShadow: =DropShadow.None
                              FillPortions: =0
                              Height: =50
                              LayoutMaxHeight: =
                              LayoutMaxWidth: =
                              RadiusBottomLeft: =0
                              RadiusBottomRight: =0
                              RadiusTopLeft: =0
                              RadiusTopRight: =0
                              Width: =Parent.Width-Self.X*2
                              X: =40
                              Y: =40
                            Children:
                              - cmd_Contracts_CommandBar:
                                  Control: CodeComponent
                                  ComponentName: cat_PowerCAT.CommandBar
                                  Properties:
                                    DisplayMode: |-
                                      =If(
                                          locContractScreenLoading,
                                          DisplayMode.Disabled,
                                          DisplayMode.Edit
                                      )
                                    Height: =40
                                    Items: |-
                                      =Table(
                                          {
                                              ItemKey: "newDevContarct",
                                              ItemDisplayName: "Add Development Contract",
                                              ItemIconName: "Add",
                                              ItemEnabled: DataSourceInfo(
                                                  'BoP Projects Contracts',
                                                  DataSourceInfo.CreatePermission
                                              )
                                          },
                                          {
                                              ItemKey: "newConstructionContarct",
                                              ItemDisplayName: "Add Construction Contract",
                                              ItemIconName: "Add",
                                              ItemEnabled: DataSourceInfo(
                                                  'BoP Projects Contracts',
                                                  DataSourceInfo.CreatePermission
                                              )
                                          },
                                          {
                                              ItemKey: "newProjecRightsContarct",
                                              ItemDisplayName: "Add Project Rights Contract",
                                              ItemIconName: "Add",
                                              ItemEnabled: DataSourceInfo(
                                                  'BoP Projects Contracts',
                                                  DataSourceInfo.CreatePermission
                                              )
                                          },
                                          {
                                              ItemKey: "editContarct",
                                              ItemDisplayName: "Edit",
                                              ItemIconName: "Edit",
                                              ItemEnabled: And(
                                                  Not(IsBlank(locSelectedContract)),
                                                  RecordInfo(
                                                      locSelectedContract,
                                                      RecordInfo.EditPermission
                                                  )
                                              )
                                          },
                                          {
                                              ItemKey: "deleteContarct",
                                              ItemDisplayName: "Delete",
                                              ItemIconName: "Delete",
                                              ItemEnabled: And(
                                                  Not(IsBlank(locSelectedContract)),
                                                  RecordInfo(
                                                      locSelectedContract,
                                                      RecordInfo.EditPermission
                                                  )
                                              )
                                          }
                                      )
                                    OnSelect: |
                                      =UpdateContext(
                                          {
                                              locContractDevCoCostsCahnged: false,
                                              locContractDevCoCostsDirty: false,
                                              locSelectedContractCostsUntilClosingDatePlan: Coalesce(
                                                  locSelectedContract.'Costs Until Closing Date Plan',
                                                  0
                                              ),
                                              locSelectedContractCostsAfterClosingDatePlan: Coalesce(
                                                  locSelectedContract.'Costs After Closing Date Plan',
                                                  0
                                              ),
                                              locSelectedContractTotalCostsCalculated: Coalesce(
                                                  locSelectedContract.'Total Costs Calculated',
                                                  0
                                              ),
                                              locSelectedContractMarginPercentage: Coalesce(
                                                  locSelectedContract.'Margin Percentage',
                                                  0
                                              ),
                                              locSelectedContractMarginFixedValues: Coalesce(
                                                  locSelectedContract.'Margin Fixed Value',
                                                  0
                                              ),
                                              locSelectedContractTotalCostOfContract: Coalesce(
                                                  locSelectedContract.'Total cost of contract [EUR]',
                                                  0
                                              )
                                          }
                                      );
                                      With(
                                          {
                                              varContractTypesChoices: 'BoP Contract Types',
                                              varColBoPContractsIds: ShowColumns(
                                                  colBoPContracts,
                                                  'BoP Projects Contracts'
                                              ),
                                              varSelectedContractId: If(
                                                  Self.Selected.ItemKey = "editContarct",
                                                  locSelectedContract.'BoP Projects Contracts',
                                                  Blank()
                                              )
                                          },
                                          ClearCollect(
                                              colSelectedConratctWithDevCoCosts,
                                              SortByColumns(
                                                  colCapexAllAccounts,
                                                  "Nummer",
                                                  SortOrder.Ascending
                                              )
                                          );
                                          If(
                                              Not(IsEmpty(varColBoPContractsIds)),
                                              ForAll(
                                                  Filter(
                                                      'BoP Contracts DevCo Costs',
                                                      ThisRecord.'BoP Contract'.'BoP Projects Contracts' in varColBoPContractsIds
                                                  ) As Source,
                                                  UpdateIf(
                                                      colSelectedConratctWithDevCoCosts,
                                                      Id = Source.'CAPEX Account'.'CAPEX Account List',
                                                      {
                                                          UsedInContractDevCo: Source.'BoP Contract'.'BoP Projects Contracts',
                                                          Used: If(
                                                              Source.'BoP Contract'.'BoP Projects Contracts' = varSelectedContractId,
                                                              false,
                                                              true
                                                          )
                                                      }
                                                  )
                                              )
                                          );
                                          Switch(
                                              Self.Selected.ItemKey,
                                              "newDevContarct",
                                              UpdateContext(
                                                  {
                                                      locSelectedContract: Blank(),
                                                      locSelectedContractFormMode: false,
                                                      locSelectedContractType: varContractTypesChoices.'Development Contract',
                                                      locIsVisibleRightPanelNewEditContract: true
                                                  }
                                              );
                                              With(
                                                  {
                                                      locBoPStandardContract: LookUp(
                                                          colBoPStandardContractsFabric,
                                                          Lower(contracttype) = Lower(Text(locSelectedContractType))
                                                      )
                                                  },
                                                  If(
                                                      !IsBlank(locBoPStandardContract),
                                                      UpdateContext(
                                                          {
                                                              locSelectedContractMarginPercentage: locBoPStandardContract.marginvalue,
                                                              locSelectedContractMarginFixedValues: locBoPStandardContract.marginvalue,
                                                              locStandardAssumptionMarginFabric: locBoPStandardContract.margin,
                                                              locStandardAssumptionMarginTypeFabric: locBoPStandardContract.margintype,
                                                              locIsStandardAssumtions: true
                                                          }
                                                      )
                                                  )
                                              );
                                              Select(but_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Buttons_Recalculate),
                                              "newConstructionContarct",
                                              UpdateContext(
                                                  {
                                                      locSelectedContract: Blank(),
                                                      locSelectedContractFormMode: false,
                                                      locSelectedContractType: varContractTypesChoices.'Construction Contract',
                                                      locIsVisibleRightPanelNewEditContract: true
                                                  }
                                              );
                                              With(
                                                  {
                                                      locBoPStandardContract: LookUp(
                                                          colBoPStandardContractsFabric,
                                                          Lower(contracttype) = Lower(Text(locSelectedContractType))
                                                      )
                                                  },
                                                  If(
                                                      !IsBlank(locBoPStandardContract),
                                                      UpdateContext(
                                                          {
                                                              locSelectedContractMarginPercentage: locBoPStandardContract.marginvalue,
                                                              locSelectedContractMarginFixedValues: locBoPStandardContract.marginvalue,
                                                              locStandardAssumptionMarginFabric: locBoPStandardContract.margin,
                                                              locStandardAssumptionMarginTypeFabric: locBoPStandardContract.margintype,
                                                              locIsStandardAssumtions: true
                                                          }
                                                      )
                                                  )
                                              );
                                              Select(but_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Buttons_Recalculate),
                                              "newProjecRightsContarct",
                                              UpdateContext(
                                                  {
                                                      locSelectedContract: Blank(),
                                                      locSelectedContractFormMode: false,
                                                      locSelectedContractType: varContractTypesChoices.'Project Rights Contract',
                                                      locIsVisibleRightPanelNewEditRightsContract: true
                                                  }
                                              ),
                                              "editContarct",
                                              With(
                                                  {
                                                      varColContractsDevCoCosts: Filter(
                                                          'BoP Contracts DevCo Costs',
                                                          ThisRecord.'BoP Contract'.'BoP Projects Contracts' = locSelectedContract.'BoP Projects Contracts'
                                                      )
                                                  },
                                                  ForAll(
                                                      varColContractsDevCoCosts As Source,
                                                      UpdateIf(
                                                          colSelectedConratctWithDevCoCosts,
                                                          Id = Source.'CAPEX Account'.'CAPEX Account List',
                                                          {
                                                              ContractDevCo: Source.'CAPEX Account'.'CAPEX Account List',
                                                              Selected: true
                                                          }
                                                      )
                                                  );
                                                  If(
                                                      locSelectedContract.'Contract Types' = 'BoP Contract Types'.'Project Rights Contract',
                                                      UpdateContext({locIsVisibleRightPanelNewEditRightsContract: true}),
                                                      UpdateContext({locIsVisibleRightPanelNewEditContract: true})
                                                  );
                                                  UpdateContext(
                                                      {
                                                          locSelectedContractType: locSelectedContract.'Contract Types',
                                                          locSelectedContractCostsUntilClosingDatePlan: locSelectedContract.'Costs Until Closing Date Plan',
                                                          locSelectedContractCostsAfterClosingDatePlan: locSelectedContract.'Costs After Closing Date Plan',
                                                          locSelectedContractFormMode: true,
                                                          locIsStandardAssumtions: If(
                                                              locSelectedContract.'Is Standard Contract',
                                                              true,
                                                              false
                                                          ),
                                                          locSelectedContractTotalCostsCalculated: locSelectedContract.'Total Costs Calculated',
                                                          locSelectedContractTotalCostOfContract: locSelectedContract.'Total cost of contract [EUR]',
                                                          locSelectedContractMarginPercentage: Value(locSelectedContract.'Margin Percentage'),
                                                          locSelectedContractMarginFixedValues: Value(locSelectedContract.'Margin Fixed Value'),
                                                          locStandardAssumptionMarginTypeFabric: Text(locSelectedContract.'Margin Type')
                                                      }
                                                  )
                                              ),
                                              "deleteContarct",
                                              UpdateContext({locIsVisiblePopUpDeleteContract: true}),
                                              ""
                                          )
                                      );
                                      Select(but_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Buttons_Reset);
                                      Select(pcf_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_RightsContract_Reset);

                                              Properties:

                                                locSelectedContractPaymentTarget.Description,
                                                With(
                                                    {
                                                        varSelectedContractPaymentTarget: First(
                                                            Filter(
                                                                Sort(
                                                                    'BoP Contracts Payment Targets',
                                                                    'Created On',
                                                                    SortOrder.Descending
                                                                ),

                                                                MatchOptions.EndsWith

                                                }

                                    }
                                );

                                    With(
                                        {
                                            varUpdatedContract: Patch(
                                                'BoP Projects Contracts',
                                                If(
                                                    IsBlank(locSelectedContract),
                                                    Defaults('BoP Projects Contracts'),
                                                    locSelectedContract
                                                ),
                                                {
                                                    Name: $"BoP RC-{gblSelectedProject.'Project ID'}-{txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Description.Value}",

                                                }
                                            )
                                        },
                                        If(
                                            IsBlank(locSelectedContract),
                                            Collect(
                                                colBoPContracts,
                                                varUpdatedContract
                                            );
                                            Patch(
                                                colBoPContracts,
                                                LookUp(
                                                    colBoPContracts,
                                                    'BoP Projects Contracts' = varUpdatedContract.'BoP Projects Contracts'
                                                ),
                                                {IsFolded: false}
                                            );
                                            UpdateContext({locSelectedContractPaymentTarget: Blank()}),
                                            Patch(
                                                colBoPContracts,
                                                LookUp(
                                                    colBoPContracts,
                                                    'BoP Projects Contracts' = varUpdatedContract.'BoP Projects Contracts'
                                                ),
                                                varUpdatedContract,
                                                {IsFolded: false}
                                            );
                                            UpdateContext({locSelectedContract: Blank()});
                                            UpdateContext(
                                                {
                                                    locSelectedContract: LookUp(
                                                        colBoPContracts,
                                                        'BoP Projects Contracts' = varUpdatedContract.'BoP Projects Contracts'
                                                    )
                                                }
                                            )
                                        )
                                    ),
                                    Notify(
                                        "Error: BoP Contract could not be saved correctly. " & "Internal error: originated on " & FirstError.Source & ". Message: " & FirstError.Message & FirstError.Details.HttpResponse,
                                        NotificationType.Error
                                    )
                                );
                                Select(but_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Buttons_Reset);
                                Reset(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_RightsContract_Content_TotalCosts);
                                Reset(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_RightsContract_Content_Description);
                                Reset(dtp_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_RightsContract_Content_ClosingDate);
                                UpdateContext(
                                    {
                                        locIsVisibleRightPanelNewEditRightsContract: false,
                                        locIsContractVisiblePopUpSpinner: false
                                    }
                                );

                              TextAlignment: ='PowerCAT.Icon.TextAlignment'.Center

                                                        Value(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_TotalCosts_Overwrite.Value),
                                                        Blank()
                                                    ),
                                                    Margin: rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Margin.Selected.Value,
                                                    'Margin Type': If(
                                                        rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginType.Visible,
                                                        rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginType.Selected.Value,
                                                        Blank()
                                                    ),
                                                    'Margin Percentage': If(
                                                        And(
                                                            rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginType.Selected.Value = 'Contract Margin Type'.Percentage,
                                                            rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginType.Visible
                                                        ),
                                                        Value(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginPercentage.Value),
                                                        Blank()
                                                    ),
                                                    'Margin Fixed Value': If(
                                                        And(
                                                            rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginType.Selected.Value = 'Contract Margin Type'.'Fixed Value',
                                                            rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginType.Visible
                                                        ),
                                                        Value(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginFixedValues.Value),
                                                        Blank()
                                                    ),
                                                    'Total cost of contract [EUR]': Value(
                                                        txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_TotalCosts_Contract.Value,
                                                        gblCurrentUser.Lang
                                                    ),
                                                    Comment: Trim(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Comment.Value),
                                                    'Owning Business Unit': gblSelectedProject.'Besitzer (Unternehmenseinheit)',
                                                    'Is Margin Standard Assumption?': locIsStandardAssumtions,
                                                    'Is Standard Contract': 'Is Standard Contract (BoP Projects Contracts)'.No
                                                }
                                            )
                                        },
                                        ClearCollect(
                                            colContractsDevCoCostsToUpdate,
                                            FirstN(
                                                'BoP Contracts DevCo Costs',
                                                0
                                            )
                                        );
                                        ForAll(
                                            Filter(
                                                colSelectedConratctWithDevCoCosts,
                                                And(
                                                    Selected = true,
                                                    Level = 3
                                                )
                                            ),
                                            Patch(
                                                colContractsDevCoCostsToUpdate,
                                                Defaults('BoP Contracts DevCo Costs'),
                                                {
                                                    Name: $"{ThisRecord.Nummer}-{ThisRecord.Name}",
                                                    'BoP Contract': varUpdatedContract,
                                                    'CAPEX Account': LookUp(
                                                        'CAPEX Account Lists' As Account,
                                                        Account.'CAPEX Account List' = ThisRecord.Id
                                                    ),
                                                    'Owning Business Unit': gblSelectedProject.'Besitzer (Unternehmenseinheit)'
                                                }
                                            )
                                        );
                                        With(
                                            {
                                                varDeletedContractDevCoCostd: RemoveIf(
                                                    'BoP Contracts DevCo Costs',
                                                    'BoP Contract'.'BoP Projects Contracts' = locSelectedContract.'BoP Projects Contracts'
                                                )
                                            },
                                            Patch(
                                                'BoP Contracts DevCo Costs',
                                                colContractsDevCoCostsToUpdate
                                            )
                                        );
                                        If(
                                            IsBlank(locSelectedContract),
                                            Sort(
                                                Collect(
                                                    colBoPContracts,
                                                    AddColumns(
                                                        varUpdatedContract,
                                                        IsFolded,
                                                        false
                                                    )
                                                ),
                                                ThisRecord.'Contract Types',
                                                SortOrder.Ascending
                                            );
                                            UpdateContext({locSelectedContractPaymentTarget: Blank()}),
                                            UpdateIf(
                                                colBoPContracts,
                                                'BoP Projects Contracts' = varUpdatedContract.'BoP Projects Contracts',
                                                AddColumns(
                                                    varUpdatedContract,
                                                    IsFolded,
                                                    false
                                                )
                                            );
                                        );
                                        UpdateContext(
                                            {
                                                locSelectedContract: LookUp(
                                                    colBoPContracts,
                                                    'BoP Projects Contracts' = varUpdatedContract.'BoP Projects Contracts'
                                                )
                                            }
                                        )
                                    ),
                                    Notify(
                                        "Error: BoP Contract could not be saved correctly. " & "Internal error: originated on " & FirstError.Source & ". Message: " & FirstError.Message & FirstError.Details.HttpResponse,
                                        NotificationType.Error
                                    )
                                );
                                Select(but_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Buttons_Reset);
                                Reset(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginPercentage);
                                Reset(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginFixedValues);
                                UpdateContext(
                                    {
                                        locSelectedContract: Blank(),
                                        locIsStandardAssumtions: false,
                                        locIsVisibleRightPanelNewEditContract: false,
                                        locIsContractVisiblePopUpSpinner: false
                                    }
                                );
                              Text: ="Save"
                              TextAlignment: ='PowerCAT.Icon.TextAlignment'.Center
                              Theme: =gblAppThemeJson
                              Tooltip: =""
                              Width: =96
                              X: =pcf_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Buttons_Cancel.X-Self.Width-20
                              Y: =Parent.Height/2-Self.Height/2
                        - but_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Buttons_Reset:
                            Control: Button@0.0.45
                            Properties:
                              Appearance: ='ButtonCanvas.Appearance'.Secondary
                              Height: =24
                              Icon: ="ArrowReset"
                              Layout: ='ButtonCanvas.Layout'.IconOnly
                              OnSelect: |
                                =Concurrent(
                                    Reset(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Description),
                                    Reset(dtp_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_ContractClosingDate),
                                    Reset(rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_CostsUntilClosingDate),
                                    Reset(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_CostsUntilClosingDate_Plan),
                                    Reset(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_CostsUntilClosingDate_Actual),
                                    Reset(rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_CostsAfterClosingDate),
                                    Reset(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_CostsAfterClosingDate_Plan),
                                    Reset(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_CostsAfterClosingDate_Actual),
                                    Reset(rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_TotalCosts),
                                    Reset(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_TotalCosts_Calculated),
                                    Reset(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_TotalCosts_Overwrite),
                                    Reset(rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Margin),
                                    Reset(rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginType),
                                    Reset(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginPercentage),
                                    Reset(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginFixedValues)
                                );
                                UpdateIf(
                                    colCapexAllAccounts,
                                    IsFolded = false,
                                    {IsFolded: true}
                                );
                                UpdateContext({locSelectedCapexAccountsForContract: Blank()});
                              Text: =
                              Visible: =false
                              Width: =24
                              X: =pcf_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Buttons_Save.X-Self.Width-20
                              Y: =Parent.Height/2-Self.Height/2
                        - but_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Buttons_Recalculate:
                            Control: Button@0.0.45
                            Properties:
                              Appearance: ='ButtonCanvas.Appearance'.Secondary
                              Icon: ="ArrowSync"
                              Layout: ='ButtonCanvas.Layout'.IconOnly
                              OnSelect: |-
                                =/*Notify(
                                    $"Recalculation started",
                                    NotificationType.Information,
                                    500
                                );*/
                                UpdateContext({locContractDevCoCostsCahnged: true});
                                With(
                                    {
                                        varSelectedContractCostsUntilClosingDatePlan: If(
                                            Not(IsBlank(dtp_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_ContractClosingDate.SelectedDate)),
                                            With(
                                                {
                                                    varColSelectedCAPEXAccounts: ShowColumns(
                                                        Filter(
                                                            colSelectedConratctWithDevCoCosts,
                                                            And(
                                                                ThisRecord.Selected = true,
                                                                ThisRecord.Level = 3
                                                            )
                                                        ),
                                                        Id
                                                    ),
                                                    varMonth: Month(dtp_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_ContractClosingDate.SelectedDate),
                                                    varYear: Year(dtp_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_ContractClosingDate.SelectedDate)
                                                },
                                                With(
                                                    {
                                                        varColSelectedCAPEXContracts: ShowColumns(
                                                            Filter(
                                                                colBoPCapexProjectContracts,
                                                                ThisRecord.Account.'CAPEX Account List' in varColSelectedCAPEXAccounts
                                                            ),
                                                            'CAPEX Project Contract'
                                                        )
                                                    },
                                                    Sum(
                                                        Filter(
                                                            colSelectedProjectCapexContractsCosts,
                                                            And(
                                                                ThisRecord.vsb_Contract.'CAPEX Project Contract' in varColSelectedCAPEXContracts,
                                                                ThisRecord.vsb_year <= varYear
                                                            )
                                                        ),
                                                        vsb_cost
                                                    ) - Sum(
                                                        Filter(
                                                            colSelectedProjectCapexContractsCosts,
                                                            And(
                                                                ThisRecord.vsb_Contract.'CAPEX Project Contract' in varColSelectedCAPEXContracts,
                                                                And(
                                                                    ThisRecord.mm > varMonth,
                                                                    ThisRecord.vsb_year = varYear
                                                                )
                                                            )
                                                        ),
                                                        vsb_cost
                                                    )
                                                )
                                            )
                                        ),
                                        varSelectedContractCostsAfterClosingDatePlan: If(
                                            Not(IsBlank(dtp_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_ContractClosingDate.SelectedDate)),
                                            With(
                                                {
                                                    varColSelectedCAPEXAccounts: ShowColumns(
                                                        Filter(
                                                            colSelectedConratctWithDevCoCosts,
                                                            And(
                                                                ThisRecord.Selected = true,
                                                                ThisRecord.Level = 3
                                                            )
                                                        ),
                                                        Id
                                                    ),
                                                    varMonth: Month(dtp_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_ContractClosingDate.SelectedDate),
                                                    varYear: Year(dtp_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_ContractClosingDate.SelectedDate)
                                                },
                                                With(
                                                    {
                                                        varColSelectedCAPEXContracts: ShowColumns(
                                                            Filter(
                                                                colBoPCapexProjectContracts,
                                                                ThisRecord.Account.'CAPEX Account List' in varColSelectedCAPEXAccounts
                                                            ),
                                                            'CAPEX Project Contract'
                                                        )
                                                    },
                                                    Sum(
                                                        Filter(
                                                            colSelectedProjectCapexContractsCosts,
                                                            And(
                                                                ThisRecord.vsb_Contract.'CAPEX Project Contract' in varColSelectedCAPEXContracts,
                                                                ThisRecord.vsb_year > varYear
                                                            )
                                                        ),
                                                        vsb_cost
                                                    ) + Sum(
                                                        Filter(
                                                            colSelectedProjectCapexContractsCosts,
                                                            And(
                                                                ThisRecord.vsb_Contract.'CAPEX Project Contract' in varColSelectedCAPEXContracts,
                                                                And(
                                                                    ThisRecord.mm > varMonth,
                                                                    ThisRecord.vsb_year = varYear
                                                                )
                                                            )
                                                        ),
                                                        vsb_cost
                                                    )
                                                )
                                            )
                                        )
                                    },
                                    With(
                                        {
                                            varSelectedContractTotalCostsCalculated: If(
                                                rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_TotalCosts.Selected.Value = 'Total Costs Type'.Calculated,
                                                Sum(
                                                    If(
                                                        rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_CostsUntilClosingDate.Selected.Value = 'Closing Date Type'.Plan,
                                                        varSelectedContractCostsUntilClosingDatePlan,
                                                        Value(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_CostsUntilClosingDate_Actual.Value)
                                                    ),
                                                    If(
                                                        rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_CostsAfterClosingDate.Selected.Value = 'Closing Date Type'.Plan,
                                                        varSelectedContractCostsAfterClosingDatePlan,
                                                        Value(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_CostsAfterClosingDate_Actual.Value)
                                                    )
                                                ),
                                                Value(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_TotalCosts_Overwrite.Value)
                                            )
                                        },
                                        UpdateContext(
                                            {
                                                locContractDevCoCostsDirty: true,
                                                locSelectedContractCostsUntilClosingDatePlan: varSelectedContractCostsUntilClosingDatePlan,
                                                locSelectedContractCostsAfterClosingDatePlan: varSelectedContractCostsAfterClosingDatePlan,
                                                locSelectedContractTotalCostsCalculated: varSelectedContractTotalCostsCalculated,
                                                locSelectedContractTotalCostOfContract: If(
                                                    rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Margin.Selected.Value = 'Margin (BoP Projects Contracts)'.Yes,
                                                    If(
                                                        rad_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginType.Selected.Value = 'Contract Margin Type'.Percentage,
                                                        If(
                                                            Or(
                                                                And(
                                                                    IsNumeric(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_TotalCosts_Calculated.Value),
                                                                    IsNumeric(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginPercentage.Value)
                                                                ),
                                                                And(
                                                                    IsNumeric(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_TotalCosts_Overwrite.Value),
                                                                    IsNumeric(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginPercentage.Value)
                                                                )
                                                            ),
                                                            Value(varSelectedContractTotalCostsCalculated) * (1 + 
                                                                Value(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginPercentage.Value)/ 100)
                                                        ),
                                                        Sum(
                                                            Value(varSelectedContractTotalCostsCalculated),
                                                            Value(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginFixedValues.Value)
                                                        )
                                                    ),
                                                    Sum(
                                                        Value(varSelectedContractTotalCostsCalculated),
                                                        Value(txt_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_MarginFixedValues.Value)
                                                    )
                                                )
                                            }
                                        )
                                    )
                                )
                              Visible: =false
                              Width: =32
                              X: =but_Contracts_RightPanel_NewEdit_Buttons_Reset.X-Self.Width-20
                              Y: =Parent.Height/2-Self.Height/2

      - cmp_OpexCosts_PopUpConfirmation_DeletePaymentTarget:
          Control: CanvasComponent
          ComponentName: cmp_PopUp_Confirmation
          Properties:
            Description: ="Are you sure that you want to permanently delete """ & locSelectedContractPaymentTarget.Description & """ payment target?"
            Height: =Parent.Height
            IconCancelButton: ="Cancel"
            IconConfirmButton: ="Delete"
            OnCancel: |-
              =UpdateContext({locIsVisiblePopUpDeleteContractPaymentTarget: false})
            OnConfirm: |
              =UpdateContext(
                  {
                      locSpinnerInformationText: "Deleting payment target...",
                      locIsVisiblePopUpSpinner: true,
                      locIsVisiblePopUpDeleteContractPaymentTarget: false
                  }
              );
              Remove(
                  'BoP Contracts Payment Targets',
                  locSelectedContractPaymentTarget
              );
              //Select(but_Contracts_BoPContracts_Refresh);
              UpdateContext({locSelectedContractPaymentTarget: Blank()});
              UpdateContext(
                  {
                      locIsVisiblePopUpSpinner: false,
                      locSpinnerInformationText: ""
                  }
              );
            TextCancelButton: ="Cancel"
            TextConfirmButton: ="Delete"
            Title: ="Delete Payment Target?"
            Visible: =locIsVisiblePopUpDeleteContractPaymentTarget
            Width: =Parent.Width
            WidthConfirmButton: =96
      - cmp_OpexCosts_PopUpConfirmation_DeleteContracts:
          Control: CanvasComponent
          ComponentName: cmp_PopUp_Confirmation
          Properties:
            Description: ="Are you sure that you want to permanently delete """ & locSelectedContract.Description & """ contract?"
            Height: =Parent.Height
            IconCancelButton: ="Cancel"
            IconConfirmButton: ="Delete"
            OnCancel: |-
              =UpdateContext({locIsVisiblePopUpDeleteContract: false})
            OnConfirm: |
              =UpdateContext(
                  {
                      locSpinnerInformationText: "Deleting contract...",
                      locIsVisiblePopUpSpinner: true,
                      locIsVisiblePopUpDeleteContract: false
                  }
              );
              Remove(
                  'BoP Projects Contracts',
                  locSelectedContract
              );
              RemoveIf(
                  colBoPContracts,
                  ThisRecord.'BoP Projects Contracts' = locSelectedContract.'BoP Projects Contracts'
              );
              UpdateContext(
                  {
                      locSelectedContract: Blank(),
                      locSelectedContractPaymentTarget: Blank()
                  }
              );
              UpdateContext(
                  {
                      locIsVisiblePopUpSpinner: false,
                      locSpinnerInformationText: ""
                  }
              );
            TextCancelButton: ="Cancel"
            TextConfirmButton: ="Delete"
            Title: ="Delete Contract?"
            Visible: =locIsVisiblePopUpDeleteContract
            Width: =Parent.Width
            WidthConfirmButton: =96
